```
28	// ---- 调试报告基础设施：错误 ring（F5 报告 errors 段唯一数据源）----
29	// kind: 0=pageerror 1=unhandledrejection 2=console.error；stack 取首行
30	interface SwErrorRecord { t: number; kind: number; msg: string; stack: string | null }
31	const ERR_RING_CAP = 50;
32	function pushSwError(kind: number, msg: unknown, stack: string | null | undefined) {
33	  const w = window as unknown as { __swErrors?: SwErrorRecord[] };
34	  if (!Array.isArray(w.__swErrors)) w.__swErrors = [];
35	  w.__swErrors.push({
36	    t: Date.now(), kind,
37	    msg: String(msg).slice(0, 500),
38	    stack: stack ? stack.split('\n')[0].slice(0, 300) : null,
39	  });
40	  if (w.__swErrors.length > ERR_RING_CAP) w.__swErrors.shift();
41	}
42	window.addEventListener('error', (e) => pushSwError(0, e.message, e.error instanceof Error ? e.error.stack : null));
43	window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection', (e) =>
44	  pushSwError(1, e.reason instanceof Error ? `${e.reason.name}: ${e.reason.message}` : e.reason, e.reason instanceof Error ? e.reason.stack : null));
45	{
46	  const origError = console.error;
47	  console.error = (...args: unknown[]) => {
48	    const err = args.find((a): a is Error => a instanceof Error);
49	    pushSwError(2, args.map((a) => (a instanceof Error ? `${a.name}: ${a.message}` : String(a))).join(' '), err ? err.stack : null);
50	    origError(...args);
51	  };
52	}
53	// 警告 ring（F5 报告 warnings 段数据源；与错误环分离——告警刷屏不能挤掉真错误）。
54	// 全量捕获 console.warn：渲染层的 warn-once（如 VanillaTiler 源矩形越界/取帧失败）
55	// 随手入环，任何模块无需单独接线
56	const WARN_RING_CAP = 50;
57	function pushSwWarn(msg: unknown) {
58	  const w = window as unknown as { __swWarns?: Array<{ t: number; msg: string }> };
59	  if (!Array.isArray(w.__swWarns)) w.__swWarns = [];
60	  w.__swWarns.push({ t: Date.now(), msg: String(msg).slice(0, 500) });
61	  if (w.__swWarns.length > WARN_RING_CAP) w.__swWarns.shift();
62	}
63	{
64	  const origWarn = console.warn;
65	  console.warn = (...args: unknown[]) => {
66	    pushSwWarn(args.map((a) => (a instanceof Error ? `${a.name}: ${a.message}` : String(a))).join(' '));
67	    origWarn(...args);
68	  };
69	}

86	  } catch {

89	  (window as unknown as { __swAtlas?: SpriteAtlas | null }).__swAtlas = atlas; // 调试/探针句柄
90	}

100	// ---- 键盘：背包/暂停/保存 ----
101	window.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
102	  const game = flow.game;
103	  if (!inGame || !game) return;
104	  // 输入框内打字不算操作键位（合成搜索框等）：除 Escape 外全部放行给输入框
105	  const tgt = e.target as HTMLElement | null;
106	  const typing = !!tgt && (tgt.tagName === 'INPUT' || tgt.tagName === 'TEXTAREA' || tgt.isContentEditable);
107	  if (typing && e.code !== 'Escape') return;
108	  switch (e.code) {
109	    case 'KeyS':
110	      // Ctrl+S 快速存档（原 F6 让位召唤面板后迁入）
111	      if (e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) {
112	        e.preventDefault();
113	        flow.doSave();
114	      }
115	      break;
116	    case 'KeyE':
117	    case 'Escape':
118	      e.preventDefault();
119	      if (game.summonPanel?.open) {
120	        // F6 召唤面板打开时,Esc 先收面板不进暂停链
121	        game.summonPanel.close();
122	        game.input.uiBlocking = false;
123	        break;
124	      }
125	      if (game.renderer.fullMap.open) {
126	        game.renderer.fullMap.open = false;
127	        break;
128	      }
129	      if (pausePanel) {
130	        pausePanel.remove();
131	        pausePanel = null;
132	        game.paused = false;
133	        ui.closeInventory();
134	        game.input.uiBlocking = false;
135	      } else if (ui.invPanel && ui.invPanel.style.display === 'block') {
136	        ui.closeInventory();
137	      } else if (e.code === 'Escape') {
138	        game.paused = true;
139	        pausePanel = ui.showPause({
140	          onResume: () => {
141	            pausePanel?.remove();
142	            pausePanel = null;
143	            game!.paused = false;
144	          },
145	          onSave: () => flow.doSave(),
146	          onExport: () => flow.doExportSave(),
147	          onSettings: () => flow.openSettings(true),
148	          onBestiary: () => flow.openBestiary(),
149	          onQuit: () => {
150	            pausePanel = null;
151	            game!.paused = false;
152	            inGame = false;
153	            flow.quitToMenu();
154	          },
155	        });
156	      } else {
157	        ui.openInventory();
158	      }
159	      break;
160	    case 'F3':
161	      e.preventDefault();
162	      game.renderer.debugMode = !game.renderer.debugMode;
163	      ui.toast(game.renderer.debugMode ? '调试面板已开启（碰撞盒高亮）' : '调试面板已关闭');
164	      break;
165	    case 'F4':
166	      e.preventDefault();
167	      game.world.explored.fill(1);
168	      game.world.exploredDirty = null; // 全图变化无脏信息 → 渲染端整幅重建
169	      game.world.exploredVersion++; // 使迷雾缓存失效重建
170	      ui.toast('已消除全图迷雾');
171	      break;
172	    case 'F5': {
173	      // 一键调试报告（机器优先 JSON）：标注系统实现保留，仅解绑 F5（可另配键）
174	      e.preventDefault();
175	      const r = downloadDebugReport(game, {
176	        playTimeMs: (flow as FlowHandle & { playStartNow: number }).playStartNow,
177	      });
178	      ui.toast(r.triggered ? `调试报告已生成：${r.filename}` : '调试报告已生成（浏览器拦截下载，可从 __swDebugReport 取）');
179	      break;
180	    }
181	    case 'F6':
182	      e.preventDefault();
183	      // 召唤面板(调试,原版无;原 F6 快速存档挪 Ctrl+S)
184	      game.toggleSummonPanel();
185	      break;
186	    case 'F7':
187	      e.preventDefault();
188	      game.forceShowWires = !game.forceShowWires;
189	      ui.toast(game.forceShowWires ? '导线显示:强制开启' : '导线显示:手持电路工具时');
190	      break;
191	    case 'F8':
192	      e.preventDefault();
193	      game.enemySpawnEnabled = !game.enemySpawnEnabled;
194	      ui.toast(game.enemySpawnEnabled ? '怪物生成已开启' : '怪物生成已关闭（已有怪物不受影响）');
195	      break;
196	    case 'F9':
197	      e.preventDefault();
198	      game.renderer.fullbright = !game.renderer.fullbright;
199	      ui.toast(game.renderer.fullbright ? '已开灯：全画面无暗影' : '已关灯：恢复正常光照');
200	      break;
201	    case 'F10':
202	      e.preventDefault();
203	      game.teleportToMouse();
204	      break;
205	    case 'KeyM':
206	      if (game.renderer.fullMap.open) {
207	        game.renderer.fullMap.open = false;
208	      } else {
209	        ui.toast(audio.toggleMute() ? '音乐已关闭' : '音乐已开启');
210	      }
211	      break;
212	  }
213	});

215	function downloadText(filename: string, text: string) {
216	  const blob = new Blob([text], { type: 'application/json' });
217	  const a = document.createElement('a');
218	  a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
219	  a.download = filename;
220	  a.click();
221	  URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href);
222	}
223	
224	// ---- 方块标注模式（F5）：标记问题方块，导出标注 + 地图给开发者 ----
225	function toggleAnnotate() {
226	  const game = flow.game;
227	  if (!game) return;
228	  game.annotateMode = !game.annotateMode;
229	  if (game.annotateMode) {
230	    ui.showAnnotatePanel({
231	      onExportMarks: exportMarks,
232	      onExportMap: exportMap,
233	      onClear: () => {
234	        if (!flow.game) return;
235	        flow.game.marks = [];
236	        ui.updateAnnotateCount(0);
237	      },
238	      onClose: () => toggleAnnotate(),
239	      onToggleFix: () => {
240	        if (!flow.game) return;
241	        flow.game.fixTexMode = !flow.game.fixTexMode;
242	        ui.setFixModeLabel(flow.game.fixTexMode);
243	        ui.toast(flow.game.fixTexMode ? '贴图纠错模式：点击方块打开其贴图表' : '已关闭贴图纠错模式');
244	      },
245	      onExportFixes: exportTexFixes,
246	    });
247	    ui.updateAnnotateCount(game.marks.length);
248	    ui.toast('标注模式已开启：点击方块标记，F5 关闭');
249	  } else {
250	    ui.closeAnnotatePanel();
251	    ui.toast('标注模式已关闭');
252	  }
253	}
254	
255	function exportMarks() {
256	  const game = flow.game;
257	  if (!game) return;
258	  const g = game;
259	  const st = g.world.store;
260	  const marks = g.marks.map((m, i) => {
261	    const idx = st.idx(m.x, m.y);
262	    const t = st.type[idx];
263	    const def = TILE_DEFS[t];
264	    const nb: Record<string, { key: string | null; fx: number; fy: number } | null> = {};
265	    const dirs: Array<[string, number, number]> = [['n', 0, -1], ['s', 0, 1], ['w', -1, 0], ['e', 1, 0],
266	      ['nw', -1, -1], ['ne', 1, -1], ['sw', -1, 1], ['se', 1, 1]];
267	    for (const [tag, dx, dy] of dirs) {
268	      const nx = m.x + dx, ny = m.y + dy;
269	      if (!st.inBounds(nx, ny)) { nb[tag] = null; continue; }
270	      const ni = st.idx(nx, ny);
271	      const nt = st.type[ni];
272	      nb[tag] = nt === 0 ? null : { key: TILE_DEFS[nt]?.key ?? String(nt), fx: st.frameX[ni], fy: st.frameY[ni] };
273	    }
274	    let sheetInfo: { sheet: number; name: string; png: string; oob: boolean } | null = null;
275	    const sheetId = def?.vanilla?.sheet;
276	    if (sheetId !== undefined && g.renderer.atlas) {
277	      const meta = g.renderer.atlas.vmeta(sheetId);
278	      const img = meta ? g.renderer.atlas.vimages.get(meta.sheet) : null;
279	      if (meta && img) {
280	        const oob = def!.vanilla!.frame === 'style'
281	          && (st.frameX[idx] + meta.grid[0] > img.width || st.frameY[idx] + meta.grid[1] > img.height);
282	        sheetInfo = { sheet: sheetId, name: meta.name, png: `${img.width}×${img.height}`, oob };
283	      }
284	    }
285	    return {
286	      no: i + 1, x: m.x, y: m.y,
287	      typeId: t, typeKey: def?.key ?? null, typeName: def?.name ?? null,
288	      frameX: st.frameX[idx], frameY: st.frameY[idx],
289	      wall: st.wall[idx],
290	      liquid: st.liquid[idx], liquidType: st.liquidType[idx],
291	      wallName: WALL_DEFS[st.wall[idx]]?.name ?? null,
292	      neighbors: nb, sheetInfo,
293	    };
294	  });
295	  const out = {
296	    worldName: g.world.name, size: [g.world.w, g.world.h],
297	    player: { x: Math.floor(g.player.x / 16), y: Math.floor(g.player.y / 16) },
298	    exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
299	    marks,
300	  };
301	  downloadText(`marks-${g.world.name.replace(/\s+/g, '_')}.json`, JSON.stringify(out, null, 2));
302	  ui.toast(`已导出 ${marks.length} 处标注`);
303	}
304	
305	function exportMap() {
306	  const game = flow.game;
307	  if (!game) return;
308	  const json = saveGameCompat(game);
309	  downloadText(`map-${game.world.name.replace(/\s+/g, '_')}.json`, json);
310	  ui.toast('已导出地图存档');
311	}
312	
313	function exportTexFixes() {
314	  const game = flow.game;
315	  if (!game) return;
316	  const fixes = ui.texFixes;
317	  if (!fixes.length) { ui.toast('还没有记录任何贴图纠错'); return; }
318	  const out = {
319	    worldName: game.world.name, size: [game.world.w, game.world.h],
320	    exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
321	    note: '每条 = 地图坐标处应使用的正确帧偏移（对应 Tiles_N 表的像素偏移）',
322	    fixes,
323	  };
324	  downloadText(`texfix-${game.world.name.replace(/\s+/g, '_')}.json`, JSON.stringify(out, null, 2));
325	  ui.toast(`已导出 ${fixes.length} 处贴图纠错`);
326	}
327	
328	function saveGameCompat(g: Game): string {
329	  return saveGame(g.world, g.player, (flow as FlowHandle & { playStartNow: number }).playStartNow, g.townNpcsForSave(), g.eventsForSave());
330	}
331	
332	// 暴露 tile/key 常量给调试控制台
333	void TILE_BY_KEY; void T; void ITEM_BY_KEY;
334	
335	// 观察进游戏事件（mainFlow 的 enterGame 发生在 Game 回调里，这里轮询 __swGame 变化）
336	let lastGameRef: Game | null = null;
337	setInterval(() => {
338	  const g = (window as unknown as { __swGame?: Game }).__swGame ?? null;
339	  if (g && g !== lastGameRef) {
340	    lastGameRef = g;
341	    inGame = true;
342	    enterGameHooks();
343	  } else if (!g && lastGameRef) {
344	    lastGameRef = null;
345	    inGame = false;
346	  }
347	}, 250);
348	
349	// ---- 启动 ----
350	kvHas('sandboxworld.quicksave').then(async () => {
351	  await loadAssets();
352	  if (atlas) UITextures.setAtlas(atlas);
353	  // options 先行(语言设置在 options 里),Lang.init 按 options.data.lang 加载语言包;
354	  // mainFlow 内的 options.load() 幂等,此处已加载则直接返回
355	  await options.load();
356	  await Promise.all([UIFont.load(), Lang.init(options.data.lang)]);
357	  VUI.init(root);
358	  VUI.startLoop(); // 自愈帧循环（HMR 杀不死，看门狗 1s 内重启）
359	  flow = createFlow(root, atlas, ui, audio);
360	
361	  // M0 验收入口：?vuidemo=1 只跑 VUI 框架演示
362	  if (new URLSearchParams(location.search).has('vuidemo')) {
363	    VUI.setState(new VuiDemoState());
364	    return;
365	  }
366	
367	  // 旁路路由：?play[=small|medium|large] 跳过主菜单直接生成世界进游戏
368	  // （主菜单 UI 改造期间绕行用；也可 ?quickload 直接读快速存档）
369	  const bootParams = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
370	  if (bootParams.has('quickload')) {
371	    void flow.quickLoad();
372	    return;
373	  }
374	  if (bootParams.has('play')) {
375	    const sz = bootParams.get('play') || 'medium';
376	    const [w, h] = sz === 'small' ? [4200, 1200] : sz === 'large' ? [8400, 2400] : [6400, 1800];
377	    void flow.newWorld('', w, h);
378	    return;
379	  }
380	
381	  flow.showTitle();
382	});
383
```

```
123	  stride: [number, number];    // 表内帧步长（grid+gap，如 [18,18]）
124	  frameSize: Array<[number, number]>; // 每个 style 的占格数

138	// NPC 贴图表（纵向帧条：小动物等）
139	export interface VanillaNpcMeta { sheet: string; frameW: number; frameH: number; count: number; }
140	export interface VanillaData {
141	  tiles: Record<string, VanillaTileMeta>;
142	  items: Record<string, VanillaItemMeta>;
143	  walls: Record<string, VanillaWallMeta>;
144	  npcs?: Record<string, VanillaNpcMeta>;
145	  tileNames?: Record<string, string>;  // 全量原版 tile id → 英文名（兼容报告用）
146	  itemNames?: Record<string, string>;
147	  /** 盔甲贴图槽位序号（Armor_Head/Armor_Armor/Armor_Legs 的索引，非物品 id） */
148	  armorIndex?: Record<string, { head: number; body: number; legs: number }>;
149	}

151	/** 整图硬 alpha：alpha ≥128 → 255，<128 → 0（并清零 RGB），消除提取 PNG 的半透明镶边 */
152	function hardAlpha(img: HTMLImageElement): HTMLCanvasElement {
153	  const c = document.createElement('canvas');
154	  c.width = img.width; c.height = img.height;
155	  const ctx = c.getContext('2d')!;
156	  ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0);
157	  const d = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, c.width, c.height);
158	  const px = d.data;
159	  for (let i = 0; i < px.length; i += 4) {
160	    if (px[i + 3] >= 128) px[i + 3] = 255;
161	    else {
162	      px[i] = 0; px[i + 1] = 0; px[i + 2] = 0; px[i + 3] = 0;
163	    }
164	  }
165	  ctx.putImageData(d, 0, 0);
166	  return c;
167	}
168	
169	export class SpriteAtlas {
170	  data = atlasJson as unknown as AtlasData;
171	  resources = resourcesJson as unknown as ResourcesData;
172	  vanilla = vanillaJson as unknown as VanillaData;
173	  images = new Map<string, HTMLImageElement | HTMLCanvasElement>();
174	  vimages = new Map<string, HTMLImageElement>(); // 原版 PNG（干净像素，不做 hardAlpha）
175	  /** UI 贴图（vanilla-ui/，干净像素不 hardAlpha——UI 有抗锯齿边缘） */
176	  uiimages = new Map<string, HTMLImageElement>();
177	  private uiFiles = (vanillaUiJson as { files: Record<string, string> }).files;
178	  /** 人工标注（annotator.html 导出）：sheet → spriteName → 方位标签 */
179	  annotations: Record<string, Record<string, string>> = {};
180	
181	  async load(onProgress?: (p: number) => void): Promise<void> {
182	    const files = Object.keys(this.data.files);
183	    // 原版 vanilla 素材与 vanilla-ui 贴图全部不在启动预载(8550 请求/主菜单 2GB 根因):
184	    // 图块/墙/NPC 表 → Game.newWorld/loadWorld 里 preloadVanillaWorld() 预载
185	    // (onWorldReady 之前完成,首帧 chunk 烘焙无回退);物品图标 → vicon 按需
186	    // 懒加载 + 进世界后 prefetchIcons() 后台补齐;UI 贴图 → vui() 按需
187	    // 懒加载(全部 11 处消费方每帧重查,首帧 null 自兜底)
188	    const vfiles: string[] = [];
189	    const uifiles: string[] = [];
190	    let done = 0;
191	    const total = files.length + vfiles.length + uifiles.length;
192	    await Promise.all([
193	      ...files.map((f) => new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
194	        const img = new Image();
195	        img.onload = () => {
196	          // 根源处理：整图硬 alpha —— 抗锯齿半透明像素（提取 PNG 的灰/黑镶边来源）
197	          // 二值化为 0/255，所有消费方（tile/墙/图标/角色）统一获得干净像素
198	          this.images.set(f, hardAlpha(img));
199	          done++;
200	          onProgress?.(done / total);
201	          resolve();
202	        };
203	        img.onerror = () => resolve();
204	        img.src = `sprites/${encodeURI(f)}`;
205	      })),
206	    ]);
207	    // 人工标注（可选，缺失时回退）
208	    try {
209	      const r = await fetch('sprites/annotations.json');
210	      if (r.ok) this.annotations = await r.json();
211	    } catch { /* 无标注 */ }
212	  }
213	
214	  // ---- 原版素材 API（无 Unity y 翻转，按 TEdit 网格寻址） ----
215	
216	  /** 原版图块元数据 */
217	  vmeta(sheetId: number): VanillaTileMeta | null {
218	    return this.vanilla.tiles[String(sheetId)] ?? null;
219	  }
220	
221	  /** 原版图块表取帧（col,row 从 0 起）。越界/缺失返回 null */
222	  vframe(sheetId: number, col: number, row: number): DrawRect | null {
223	    const m = this.vmeta(sheetId);
224	    if (!m) return null;
225	    const img = this.ensureVImage(m.sheet);  // 懒加载兜底(正常路径 preloadVanillaWorld 已就绪)
226	    if (!img) return null;
227	    if (col < 0 || row < 0 || col >= m.cols || row >= m.rows) return null;
228	    return { img, sx: col * m.stride[0], sy: row * m.stride[1], sw: m.grid[0], sh: m.grid[1] };
229	  }
230	
231	  /** 原版表内任意像素偏移取帧（style/显式帧：18px 步长的 frameX/frameY 直用） */
232	  vframeAt(sheetId: number, fx: number, fy: number): DrawRect | null {
233	    const m = this.vmeta(sheetId);
234	    if (!m) return null;
235	    // 与 vframe/vrect 同语义懒加载（ensureVImage）：place_v_* 物品图标走本方法,
236	    // 此前直接 vimages.get——表未载时不发加载请求,宝箱内家具类物品图标永久回退
237	    const img = this.ensureVImage(m.sheet);
238	    if (!img) return null;
239	    if (fx < 0 || fy < 0 || fx + m.grid[0] > img.width || fy + m.grid[1] > img.height) return null;
240	    return { img, sx: fx, sy: fy, sw: m.grid[0], sh: m.grid[1] };
241	  }
242	
243	  /** 原版表内任意矩形（多格物体整体取图，如墓碑 2×2 = 34×34px） */
244	  vrect(sheetId: number, fx: number, fy: number, w: number, h: number): DrawRect | null {
245	    const m = this.vmeta(sheetId);
246	    if (!m) return null;
247	    const img = this.ensureVImage(m.sheet);
248	    if (!img) return null;
249	    if (fx < 0 || fy < 0 || fx + w > img.width || fy + h > img.height) return null;
250	    return { img, sx: fx, sy: fy, sw: w, sh: h };
251	  }
252	
253	  /** 原版 NPC 贴图表取帧（纵向帧条，frameIdx 0-based）。
254	   *  未登记的 id 懒加载 vanilla/NPC_{id}.png（帧数来自 vanilla-npcs.json），首帧返回 null 下一帧生效 */
255	  private lazyNpcMeta = new Map<string, VanillaNpcMeta>();
256	  /** ⚠仅适用【纵向帧条】NPC 表。横向变体横条 NPC（如 594 风气球 = 8 列×32px 变体,
257	   *  Main.cs:23383 Frame(8,1,ai[2])）走此路径会把整条横排画出来——此类 NPC 必须
258	   *  在 Renderer.drawEnemy 加专属分支按列切片（见 drawWindyBalloon）。 */
259	  vnpc(npcId: number, frameIdx: number): DrawRect | null {
260	    let m: VanillaNpcMeta | undefined = this.vanilla.npcs?.[String(npcId)];
261	    if (!m) {
262	      const key = String(npcId);
263	      m = this.lazyNpcMeta.get(key);
264	      if (!m) {
265	        const sheet = `vanilla/NPC_${npcId}.png`;
266	        const img = this.vimages.get(sheet);
267	        if (!img) {
268	          const im = new Image();
269	          im.onload = () => this.vimages.set(sheet, im);
270	          im.src = `sprites/${sheet}`;
271	          return null;
272	        }
273	        const frames = (vanillaNpcFrames as Record<string, number>)[key] ?? 1;
274	        const fh = Math.max(1, Math.floor(img.height / frames));
275	        const meta: VanillaNpcMeta = { sheet, frameW: img.width, frameH: fh, count: frames };
276	        this.lazyNpcMeta.set(key, meta);
277	        m = meta;
278	      }
279	    }
280	    const img = this.vimages.get(m.sheet);
281	    if (!img) return null;
282	    const idx = Math.max(0, Math.min(m.count - 1, frameIdx));
283	    return { img, sx: 0, sy: idx * m.frameH, sw: m.frameW, sh: m.frameH };
284	  }
285	
286	  /** 原版 tile/item 英文名（全量表，未白名单的也有） */
287	  vTileName(id: number): string | null { return this.vanilla.tileNames?.[String(id)] ?? null; }
288	  vItemName(id: number): string | null { return this.vanilla.itemNames?.[String(id)] ?? null; }
289	
290	  vnpcMeta(npcId: number): VanillaNpcMeta | null {
291	    return this.vanilla.npcs?.[String(npcId)] ?? null;
292	  }
293	
294	  /** 原版杂项单图（呼吸气泡等） */
295	  vmisc(path: string): DrawRect | null {
296	    const img = this.vimages.get(path);
297	    if (!img) return null;
298	    return { img, sx: 0, sy: 0, sw: img.width, sh: img.height };
299	  }
300	
301	  /** UI 贴图（vanilla-ui/ 命名空间）。name 为白名单键，如 'UI_PanelBackground'。
302	   *  按需懒加载(消费方每帧重查,未就绪返回 null 自兜底) */
303	  vui(name: string): DrawRect | null {
304	    const path = this.uiFiles[name];
305	    if (!path) return null;
306	    const img = this.ensureUiImage(path);
307	    if (!img) return null;
308	    return { img, sx: 0, sy: 0, sw: img.width, sh: img.height };
309	  }
310	
311	  private _uiPending = new Set<string>();
312	  private ensureUiImage(file: string): HTMLImageElement | null {
313	    const hit = this.uiimages.get(file);
314	    if (hit) return hit;
315	    if (this._uiPending.has(file)) return null;
316	    this._uiPending.add(file);
317	    const im = new Image();
318	    im.onload = () => { this.uiimages.set(file, im); this._uiPending.delete(file); };
319	    im.onerror = () => { this._uiPending.delete(file); };
320	    im.src = `sprites/${encodeURI(file)}`;
321	    return null;
322	  }
323	
324	  /** 原版物品图标。构建期已 shelf-pack 进少数 Item_Atlas_k.png 图集(ix/iy/iw/ih
325	   *  子矩形,见 scripts/vanilla-atlas.mjs);旧单体条目(无矩形字段)回退整图语义。
326	   *  未加载时触发后台懒加载并返回 null(下帧生效) */
327	  vicon(itemId: number): DrawRect | null {
328	    const m = this.vanilla.items[String(itemId)];
329	    if (!m) return null;
330	    const img = this.ensureVImage(m.icon);
331	    if (!img) return null;
332	    if (m.ix !== undefined && m.iw !== undefined && m.ih !== undefined) {
333	      return { img, sx: m.ix, sy: m.iy ?? 0, sw: m.iw, sh: m.ih };
334	    }
335	    return { img, sx: 0, sy: 0, sw: img.width, sh: img.height };
336	  }
337	
338	  private _iconPending = new Set<string>();
339	  private _worldPreloaded = false;
340	  /** 懒加载完成回调(Game 注册 → ChunkCache 全量标脏:晚到的表重新烘焙
341	   *  已缓存的 chunk,否则 fallback 会永久烤进 canvas) */
342	  onVImageLoaded: ((file: string) => void) | null = null;
343	
344	  /** 预载文件清单(去重+decode)。tile/wall/NPC/misc 表与图标的统一底层 */
345	  async preloadFiles(files: Iterable<string>, onProgress?: (p: number) => void): Promise<void> {
346	    const list = [...new Set(files)];
347	    let done = 0;
348	    await Promise.all(list.map((f) => new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
349	      if (this.vimages.has(f)) { done++; onProgress?.(done / list.length); return resolve(); }
350	      const img = new Image();
351	      img.onload = () => {
352	        this.vimages.set(f, img);
353	        // decode():预载阶段完成解码,否则 Chrome 延迟到首次 draw 才解码,
354	        // 大表(2000px 级)首帧仍会卡一拍——"预载"语义要求字节+解码双就绪
355	        img.decode().then(() => { done++; onProgress?.(done / list.length); resolve(); }, () => resolve());
356	        this.onVImageLoaded?.(f);
357	      };
358	      img.onerror = () => resolve();
359	      img.src = `sprites/${encodeURI(f)}`;
360	    })));
361	  }
362	
363	  /** 按图块 sheet id + 墙 id 预载对应贴图表。
364	   *  Game 用出生点区域类型扫描调用——只载画面涉及的表(出生点半径内实测仅
365	   *  22/378 张图块表),而不是全量 ~750 张(~250MB 解码) */
366	  preloadTileSheetsFor(tileSheets: Iterable<number>, wallIds: Iterable<number>): Promise<void> {
367	    const files = new Set<string>();
368	    for (const id of tileSheets) {
369	      const m = this.vanilla.tiles[String(id)];
370	      if (m) files.add(m.sheet);
371	    }
372	    for (const id of wallIds) {
373	      const m = this.vanilla.walls[String(id)];
374	      if (m) files.add(m.sheet);
375	    }
376	    return this.preloadFiles(files);
377	  }
378	
379	  /** 预载常驻杂项(树冠/液体/瀑布/电路)+ NPC 表(小动物)——出生点必有,量小全载 */
380	  preloadMiscAndNpcs(): Promise<void> {
381	    return this.preloadFiles([
382	      ...VANILLA_MISC,
383	      ...Object.values(this.vanilla.npcs ?? {}).map((n) => n.sheet),
384	    ]);
385	  }
386	
387	  /** 预载世界渲染所需原版表(全量,~750 张)。仅调试/兜底用;正常路径走
388	   *  preloadTileSheetsFor + preloadMiscAndNpcs 的出生点子集 */
389	  async preloadVanillaWorld(): Promise<void> {
390	    if (this._worldPreloaded) return;
391	    this._worldPreloaded = true;
392	    await Promise.all([
393	      this.preloadTileSheetsFor(
394	        Object.keys(this.vanilla.tiles).map(Number),
395	        Object.keys(this.vanilla.walls).map(Number),
396	      ),
397	      this.preloadMiscAndNpcs(),
398	    ]);
399	  }
400	  /** 按需加载 vanilla 单图(去重;失败静默)。命中返回元素,否则 null。
401	   *  渲染路径一律用此入口(经 vimg 别名)——直接 vimages.get 会绕过懒加载与
402	   *  onVImageLoaded 重烘焙钩子(棕榈树干传送消失的根因) */
403	  ensureVImage(file: string): HTMLImageElement | null {
404	    const hit = this.vimages.get(file);
405	    if (hit) return hit;
406	    if (this._iconPending.has(file) || this._vImageFailed.has(file)) return null;
407	    this._iconPending.add(file);
408	    const im = new Image();
409	    im.onload = () => {
410	      this.vimages.set(file, im);
411	      this._iconPending.delete(file);
412	      this._vImageFailed.delete(file);
413	      this.onVImageLoaded?.(file);  // 晚到的表 → ChunkCache 重烘焙
414	    };
415	    im.onerror = () => {
416	      this._iconPending.delete(file);
417	      this._vImageFailed.add(file);   // 永久失败:不再重复请求,供消费方分级告警
418	    };
419	    im.src = `sprites/${encodeURI(file)}`;
420	    return null;
421	  }
422	  /** 表是否已确认加载失败(404 等)——与"加载中瞬态"区分 */
423	  vImageFailed(file: string): boolean { return this._vImageFailed.has(file); }
424	  private _vImageFailed = new Set<string>();
425	  /** 后台预取全部物品图标(保留兼容入口;预载流程请用 awaited 的 preloadIcons) */
426	  prefetchIcons(): void {
427	    void this.preloadIcons();
428	  }
429	
430	  private _iconsPromise: Promise<void> | null = null;
431	  /** 预载全部物品图标素材。图集化后清单 = 去重后的 ~3 张 Item_Atlas_k.png
432	   *  (此前 6059 张单体逐张请求);缓存 Promise——并发 await 的调用者
433	   *  都会等到同一批加载完成(此前旗标早退会让第二个调用者拿到假完成) */
434	  preloadIcons(onProgress?: (p: number) => void): Promise<void> {
435	    if (this._iconsPromise) return this._iconsPromise;
436	    const icons = [...new Set(Object.values(this.vanilla.items).map((m) => m.icon))];
437	    let done = 0;
438	    this._iconsPromise = Promise.all(icons.map((f) => new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
439	      if (this.vimages.has(f)) { done++; onProgress?.(done / icons.length); return resolve(); }
440	      const im = new Image();
441	      im.onload = () => { this.vimages.set(f, im); done++; onProgress?.(done / icons.length); resolve(); };
442	      im.onerror = () => resolve();
443	      im.src = `sprites/${encodeURI(f)}`;
444	    }))).then(() => undefined);
445	    return this._iconsPromise;
446	  }
447	
448	  /** 预载 UI 贴图按 key 前缀(如 ['Player_'] = 纸娃娃身体/发型,545 张)。 */
449	  async preloadUiPrefix(prefixes: string[], onProgress?: (p: number) => void): Promise<void> {
450	    const files = Object.entries(this.uiFiles)
451	      .filter(([k]) => prefixes.some((p) => k.startsWith(p)))
452	      .map(([, f]) => f);
453	    return this.preloadUiFiles(files, onProgress);
454	  }
455	
456	  /** 按 UI 文件路径预载(装备中的具体 Armor 表;decode 保证首帧无解码卡顿) */
457	  async preloadUiFiles(files: Iterable<string>, onProgress?: (p: number) => void): Promise<void> {
458	    const list = [...new Set(files)];
459	    let done = 0;
460	    await Promise.all(list.map((f) => new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
461	      if (this.uiimages.has(f)) { done++; onProgress?.(done / list.length); return resolve(); }
462	      const im = new Image();
463	      im.onload = () => {
464	        this.uiimages.set(f, im);
465	        im.decode().then(() => { done++; onProgress?.(done / list.length); resolve(); }, () => resolve());
466	      };
467	      im.onerror = () => resolve();
468	      im.src = `sprites/${encodeURI(f)}`;
469	    })));
470	  }
471	
472	  has(file: string): boolean {
473	    return this.images.has(file);
474	  }
475	
476	  /** 取精灵绘制矩形（Unity y 翻转已处理）。找不到返回 null。 */
477	  rect(file: string, sprite: string): DrawRect | null {
478	    const img = this.images.get(file);
479	    const entry = this.data.files[file];
480	    if (!img || !entry) return null;
481	    const s = entry.sprites.find((x) => x.name === sprite);
482	    if (!s) return null;
483	    return { img, sx: s.x, sy: img.height - s.y - s.h, sw: s.w, sh: s.h };
484	  }
485	
486	  animFrames(anim: string): DrawRect[] {
487	    const frames = this.resources.anims[anim] ?? [];
488	    return frames.map((f) => this.rect(f.file, f.sprite)).filter(Boolean) as DrawRect[];
489	  }
490	}

492	// ---- 物品图标：一律原版 Item_N（旧 Maples UI/ 图标层已整体移除） ----
493	import { TILE_BY_KEY, TILE_DEFS } from '../data/tiles';
494	
495	/** DrawAnimationVertical(int.MaxValue, 3) 的静态等价(IsFood 全表) */
496	const FOOD = { dur: Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER, n: 3, static: true };
497	const FOOD_IDS: number[] = [
498	  353, 357, 967, 969, 1787, 1911, 1912, 1919, 1920, 2266, 2267, 2268, 
499	  2425, 2426, 2427, 3195, 3532, 4009, 4010, 4011, 4012, 4013, 4014, 4015, 
500	  4016, 4017, 4018, 4019, 4020, 4021, 4022, 4023, 4024, 4025, 4026, 4027, 
501	  4028, 4029, 4030, 4031, 4032, 4033, 4034, 4035, 4036, 4037, 4282, 4283, 
502	  4284, 4285, 4286, 4287, 4288, 4289, 4290, 4291, 4292, 4293, 4294, 4295, 
503	  4296, 4297, 4403, 4411, 4614, 4615, 4616, 4617, 4618, 4619, 4620, 4621, 
504	  4622, 4623, 4624, 4625, 5009, 5041, 5042, 5092, 5093, 5275, 5277, 5278, 
505	  5537, 5645, 
506	];
507	
508	/**
509	 * 物品贴图动画注册表(Main.InitializeItemAnimations :3685-3722 1:1)。
510	 * 食物族:ItemID.Sets.IsFood(:258,86 项)全部为竖 3 帧条,原版注册
511	 * DrawAnimationVertical(int.MaxValue,3) = 恒帧 0(顶部一帧);本表以 FOOD
512	 * 静态条目等价表达(切片帧高 = 总高/3)。
513	 * 物品源图是竖排多帧条(如坠星 Item_75=22×208),不切片会整条入画。
514	 * dur=tick/帧;pingPong=往返;static=true=恒帧 0(IsFood 的 int.MaxValue
515	 * 静态三帧与 NotActuallyAnimating 族)。
516	 */
517	export const ITEM_ANIMATION: Record<number, { dur: number; n: number; pingPong?: boolean; static?: boolean }> = {
518	  3581: { dur: 4, n: 4 },
519	  3580: { dur: 6, n: 4 },
520	  75: { dur: 5, n: 8, pingPong: true },    // 坠星 8 帧 PingPong 旋转
521	  575: { dur: 6, n: 4 }, 547: { dur: 6, n: 4 }, 520: { dur: 6, n: 4 },
522	  548: { dur: 6, n: 4 }, 521: { dur: 6, n: 4 }, 549: { dur: 6, n: 4 },
523	  3453: { dur: 6, n: 4 }, 3454: { dur: 6, n: 4 }, 3455: { dur: 6, n: 4 },
524	  4068: { dur: 6, n: 4, static: true },   // NotActuallyAnimating(:3701-3709)
525	  4069: { dur: 6, n: 4, static: true },
526	  4070: { dur: 6, n: 4, static: true },
527	  5644: { dur: 7, n: 9 },                 // ScryingOrb(专属着色器,垂直循环近似)
528	  // ---- 食物族(IsFood :258):竖 3 帧条,原版 int.MaxValue 恒帧 0 ----
529	  ...Object.fromEntries(FOOD_IDS.map((v) => [v, FOOD])),
530	};
531	
532	
533	/** 物品动画当前帧(DrawAnimationVertical.GetFrame 语义;tick=游戏 tick 或等价毫秒换算) */
534	export function itemAnimFrame(vid: number, tick: number): number {
535	  const a = ITEM_ANIMATION[vid];
536	  if (!a || a.static) return 0;
537	  const span = a.pingPong ? a.n * 2 - 2 : a.n;
538	  const idx = Math.floor(tick / a.dur) % span;
539	  return a.pingPong && idx >= a.n ? span - idx : idx;
540	}
541	
542	/** 把整条 DrawRect 按帧切片(vid 无动画原样返回) */
543	export function sliceItemAnimFrame(vid: number, ar: DrawRect, tick: number): DrawRect {
544	  const a = ITEM_ANIMATION[vid];
545	  if (!a || a.n <= 1) return ar;
546	  const fh = ar.sh / a.n;
547	  const f = Math.min(a.n - 1, itemAnimFrame(vid, tick));
548	  return { img: ar.img, sx: ar.sx, sy: ar.sy + Math.round(fh * f), sw: ar.sw, sh: Math.round(fh) };
549	}
550	
551	export function atlasIconForKey(atlas: SpriteAtlas, key: string): DrawRect | null {
552	  const vid = VANILLA_ITEM_ICON_MAP[key];
553	  if (vid !== undefined) return atlas.vicon(vid);
554	  // 阶段 5:vi_<id>_* 全量物品回退原版图标
555	  if (key.startsWith('vi_')) {
556	    const id = parseInt(key.slice(3), 10);
557	    if (Number.isFinite(id)) return atlas.vicon(id);
558	  }
559	  // 阶段 5:place_v_* 放置物品回退用图块贴图首帧作图标
560	  if (key.startsWith('place_v_')) {
561	    const tk = key.slice('place_'.length);
562	    const tid = TILE_BY_KEY[tk];
563	    if (tid !== undefined) {
564	      const td = TILE_DEFS[tid];
565	      // vframeAt 而非 vframe:窄条表(如压板 135 的 16×200 竖条)cols=0 会让 vframe 判越界
566	      if (td?.vanilla) return atlas.vframeAt(td.vanilla.sheet, 0, 0) ?? atlas.vframe(td.vanilla.sheet, 0, 0);
567	    }
568	  }
569	  return null;
570	}
```

```
55	export function createFlow(root: HTMLElement, atlas: SpriteAtlas | null, ui: UI, audio: AudioSystem): FlowHandle {
56	  let game: Game | null = null;
57	  let mobile: MobileControls | null = null;
58	  (window as unknown as { __swAudio?: AudioSystem }).__swAudio = audio; // 探针调试桥

65	  void options.load();
66	  options.onChange((d) => {
67	    audio.setVolume(d.musicVol);
68	    UISfx.sfx.master = d.sfxVol;
69	    UIScale.userScale = d.uiScale;
70	    devMode = d.devMode;

82	  (window as unknown as { __swLoadJson?: (t: string) => Promise<void> }).__swLoadJson = (t: string) => loadFromJson(t);
83	  const fileInput = document.createElement('input');
84	  fileInput.type = 'file';
85	  fileInput.accept = '.json';
86	  fileInput.style.display = 'none';
87	  root.appendChild(fileInput);
88	  const wldInput = document.createElement('input');
89	  wldInput.type = 'file';

91	  wldInput.style.display = 'none';
92	  root.appendChild(wldInput);

95	
96	  function enterGame(g: Game) {
97	    game = g;
98	    (window as unknown as { __swGame: Game }).__swGame = g;
99	    (window as unknown as { __swUI: UI }).__swUI = ui; // 探针/控制台直调(成就弹窗预览等)
100	    // 移动端：虚拟控件层（触屏设备启用；桌面零渲染零影响）——在世界触摸的
101	    // 用户手势内尝试全屏+横屏锁定（ⓞ 进世界点击即手势；失败静默，⛶ 按钮兜底）
102	    if (isTouchDevice()) {
103	      mobile?.destroy();
104	      mobile = new MobileControls(g, ui.root);
105	      void tryFullscreenLandscape();
106	    }
107	    // HMR 双实例检测（F5 调试报告 instance 段）：每次挂载计数 +1，>1 即模块分叉
108	    (window as unknown as { __swInstanceCount?: number }).__swInstanceCount =
109	      ((window as unknown as { __swInstanceCount?: number }).__swInstanceCount ?? 0) + 1;
110	    // E2E/控制台调试:tile key → 内部 id 反查(测试脚本放置图块用)
111	    (window as unknown as { __swTileByKey?: (k: string) => number }).__swTileByKey = (k: string) =>
112	      (TILE_BY_KEY as Record<string, number>)[k] ?? -1;
113	    // E2E 调试:内部 id → def 关键字段(注册表漂移排查)
114	    (window as unknown as { __swTileDefById?: (id: number) => unknown }).__swTileDefById = (id: number) => {
115	      const d = (TILE_DEFS as Array<{ key: string; vanilla?: { sheet: number; frame: string; fw?: number; fh?: number } }>)[id];
116	      return d ? { key: d.key, sheet: d.vanilla?.sheet, frame: d.vanilla?.frame, fw: d.vanilla?.fw, fh: d.vanilla?.fh } : null;
117	    };
118	    // E2E/控制台调试:直接加载存档 JSON 文本(绕过设置面板的 file input)
119	    // (挂模块级而非 enterGame:菜单阶段测试脚本就要用)
120	    // 液体浸润实验台:?liquidlab 参数 / window.__swLiquidLab() 控制台命令
121	    (window as unknown as { __swLiquidLab?: () => void }).__swLiquidLab = () => {
122	      liquidLab(g);

124	    if (new URLSearchParams(location.search).has('liquidlab')) {
125	      setTimeout(() => (window as unknown as { __swLiquidLab?: () => void }).__swLiquidLab?.(), 1500);
126	    }

130	    stopMenu();
131	    titleMenu?.destroy();
132	    titleMenu = null;
133	    ui.game = g;

135	    g.start();
136	    audio.play('main');
137	    ui.toast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.Welcome', g.world.name));
138	  }

141	    if (!devMode) return;

145	    g.world.exploredVersion++;
146	  }

148	  function makeGame(): Game {
149	    const g = new Game(root, {
150	      onWorldReady: () => { ui.hideProgress(); enterGame(g); maybeDev(g); applyAppearance(g); },
151	      onInventoryChanged: () => ui.refreshAll(),
152	      onBuffsChanged: () => ui.refreshBuffs(),
153	      onToast: (m) => ui.toast(m),
154	      onAchievementPopup: (name, title) => ui.achievementPopup(name, title),
155	      // 原版 Main.NewText 消息列(Main.cs:64095 → LegacyChatMonitor)；
156	      // width = NewTextMultiline WidthLimit（广播盒 460px 折行，-1 不限）
157	      onChat: (t, r, g, b, width) => ui.chatMessage(t, r, g, b, width),
158	      // NPC 对话系统(SetTalkNPC + GetChat)
159	      onNpcDialog: (name, chat, buttons, portrait) => ui.showNpcDialog(name, chat, buttons, portrait),
160	      onNpcDialogClose: () => ui.closeNpcDialog(),
161	      onReforgeOpen: () => ui.showReforge(),
162	      onNpcShop: (title, items, copper, happinessMul) => ui.showNpcShop(title, items, copper, happinessMul),
163	      // NPC 快乐度详情浮层（ReportHappiness.Interact）
164	      onNpcHappiness: (info) => ui.showNpcHappiness(info),
165	      onReadSign: (text) => ui.showSign(text),
166	      onEditSign: (x, y, text) => ui.editSign(x, y, text),
167	      onDayNight: (isDay) => audio.setDayNight(isDay),
168	      onMusic: (id) => audio.playMusic(id),
169	      // 硬核角色死亡消亡（Player.cs:38255 HardcoreDeathPenalty）：标记回写角色存档，
170	      // 重进角色列表即灰显禁入（原版 KillMeForGood :38173+ 直接删 .plr 档）
171	      onHardcoreDeath: async (appearance) => {
172	        selectedAppearance = appearance;
173	        if (selectedCharId != null) await charStore.update(selectedCharId, appearance);
174	      },
175	      // 梳妆台更衣窗（Main.OpenClothesWindow Main.cs:43568）：确认后回写角色档 +
176	      // 在线外观（原版外观随 .plr 档，CancelClothesWindow 取消则回退六色快照）
177	      onClothesWindow: (appearance) => {
178	        ui.showClothesWindow(appearance, async (a) => {
179	          selectedAppearance = a;
180	          g.player.appearance = a;
181	          if (typeof a.team === 'number') g.player.team = a.team;
182	          g.net?.resendAppearance();
183	          if (selectedCharId != null) await charStore.update(selectedCharId, a);
184	          ui.toast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.ClothesWindow.Changed'));
185	        });
186	      },
187	    }, atlas);
188	    return g;
189	  }

191	  // ---- 世界流程 ----
192	
193	  async function newWorld(seed: string, w: number, h: number) {
194	    const g = makeGame();
195	    ui.showProgress(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Progress.GeneratingWorld'), 0.05);
196	    await g.newWorld(seed || String(Date.now()), w, h, (label, p) => ui.showProgress(Lang.worldgenText(label), p));
197	  }
198	
199	  /** 把选中角色的外观应用到玩家（进游戏后调用）。联机时补发 SyncPlayer——
200	   *  初始两发（PlayerSlot/PlayerSpawn 时刻）都在外观应用前，远端只见默认皮肤 */
201	  function applyAppearance(g: Game) {
202	    if (selectedAppearance) {
203	      g.player.appearance = selectedAppearance;
204	      if (typeof selectedAppearance.team === 'number') g.player.team = selectedAppearance.team;
205	      g.net?.resendAppearance();
206	    }
207	  }
208	
209	  async function quickLoad() {
210	    if (!quickSaveExists) { ui.toast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.NoQuickSave')); return; }
211	    await loadFromKey(QUICK_SAVE_KEY);
212	  }
213	
214	  /** 玩家状态回填（worker/主线程两路共用） */
215	  function applyPlayer(g: Game, player: ReturnType<typeof loadSaveData>['player']) {
216	    g.player.hp = player.hp;
217	    g.player.x = player.x;
218	    g.player.y = player.y;
219	    // 上限扩容进度（水晶之心/生命果/魔力水晶；旧档缺省 100/20/20）
220	    // 永久升级族（旧档缺省全 false）
221	    const pu = player.permanentUpgrades;
222	    if (pu) {
223	      if (pu.extraAccessory !== undefined) g.player.inv.extraAccessory = pu.extraAccessory;
224	      if (pu.usedAegisCrystal !== undefined) g.player.usedAegisCrystal = pu.usedAegisCrystal;
225	      if (pu.usedAegisFruit !== undefined) g.player.usedAegisFruit = pu.usedAegisFruit;
226	      if (pu.usedArcaneCrystal !== undefined) g.player.usedArcaneCrystal = pu.usedArcaneCrystal;
227	      if (pu.usedGummyWorm !== undefined) g.player.usedGummyWorm = pu.usedGummyWorm;
228	      if (pu.usedAmbrosia !== undefined) g.player.usedAmbrosia = pu.usedAmbrosia;
229	    }
230	    if (player.baseMaxHp !== undefined) g.player.baseMaxHp = player.baseMaxHp;
231	    if (player.baseMaxMana !== undefined) g.player.baseMaxMana = player.baseMaxMana;
232	    if (player.mana !== undefined) g.player.mana = player.mana;
233	    // 背包布局迁移（旧 54 槽自创布局 → 原版 58 槽+armor[20]；Inventory.migrateLegacy 判别）
234	    const mig = Inventory.migrateLegacy(player.inventory);
235	    g.player.inv.slots = mig.slots;
236	    if (player.armor) g.player.inv.armor = player.armor.map((it) => it ? { ...it } : null);
237	    if (player.dye) g.player.inv.dye = player.dye.map((it) => it ? { ...it } : null);
238	    if (player.trash) g.player.inv.trash = { ...player.trash };
239	    // 杂项装备/染料 + 三套 Loadout 回填（旧档缺省全空）
240	    if (player.miscEquips) g.player.inv.miscEquips = player.miscEquips.map((it) => it ? { ...it } : null);
241	    if (player.miscDyes) g.player.inv.miscDyes = player.miscDyes.map((it) => it ? { ...it } : null);
242	    if (player.loadouts) {
243	      for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
244	        const src = player.loadouts[k];
245	        if (!src) continue;
246	        g.player.inv.loadouts[k] = {
247	          armor: src.armor.map((it) => it ? { ...it } : null),
248	          dye: src.dye.map((it) => it ? { ...it } : null),
249	        };
250	      }
251	    }
252	    if (player.currentLoadout !== undefined) g.player.inv.currentLoadout = player.currentLoadout;
253	    g.player.inv.selected = player.selected;
254	    // 玩家储物×4 回填（29/97/463/491；旧档缺省全空）
255	    if (player.banks) {
256	      for (let b = 0; b < 4; b++) {
257	        const src = player.banks[b] ?? [];
258	        g.player.banks[b] = src.concat(Array(Math.max(0, 40 - src.length)).fill(null)).slice(0, 40);
259	      }
260	    }
261	    // Buff 槽（PL:53913-53925;Main.buffNoSave 已在序列化侧过滤）
262	    g.player.buffs.applySaved(player.buffs);
263	    if (player.taxMoney !== undefined) g.player.taxMoney = player.taxMoney;   // PL:53839
264	    if (player.golferScoreAccumulated) g.player.golferScoreAccumulated = player.golferScoreAccumulated; // PL:1792
265	    if (player.deathsPve !== undefined) g.player.deathsPve = player.deathsPve; // PL:53840
266	    if (player.anglerQuestsFinished !== undefined) g.player.anglerQuestsFinished = player.anglerQuestsFinished; // PL:1790
267	    // ★ 读档回填后必须刷 UI：HUD 快捷栏在 makeGame 时以空背包画过一次，
268	    //   此前不刷新 → 存档道具要等用户点工具栏/开背包才显示
269	    g.cb.onInventoryChanged();
270	  }
271	
272	  /** 旧档无 header.difficulty（本次修复前创建的专家/大师世界）→ 以槽位
273	   *  WorldMeta.difficulty 回填 world.difficulty；新档以 header 为权威跳过 */
274	  let difficultyFallback: number | null = null;
275	  /** 原版 loadWorld 进度文案(WorldFile.cs):tiles=gen[51] "正在加载世界数据: X%"
276	   *  按列(:2514-2518);settle=gen[27] "正在安置液体 X%" 收敛比例 50-100%(:762);
277	   *  其余阶段保持自定义 LoadingSave */
278	  function loadProgressLabel(phase: string, rawP: number): string {
279	    const p = Number.isFinite(rawP) ? rawP : 0;   // NaN 防御(worker 瞬断/旧缓存事件)
280	    if (phase === 'tiles') {
281	      return Lang.text('LegacyWorldGen.51') + ' ' + Math.min(100, Math.floor(p * 100) + 1) + '%';
282	    }
283	    if (phase === 'settle') {
284	      return Lang.text('LegacyWorldGen.27') + ' ' + Math.min(100, Math.floor(50 + p * 50)) + '%';
285	    }
286	    return Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Progress.LoadingSave');
287	  }
288	
289	  function applyDifficultyFallback(world: import('./world/World').World, save?: { header?: { difficulty?: number } }) {
290	    if (difficultyFallback == null || save?.header?.difficulty !== undefined) return;
291	    world.difficulty = difficultyFallback;
292	  }
293	
294	  /** 按 IDB key 读档：主路径 worker 内直读 IDB（免大 JSON 字符串结构化克隆到
295	   *  worker 的主线程序列化开销——大存档实测秒级 100% CPU）；worker 不可用时
296	   *  才在主线程 kvGet 走 fallback */
297	  async function loadFromKey(key: string) {
298	    try {
299	      // worker 路径：IDB 读取 + JSON 解析 + RLE 解码 + load 模式沉降全在后台
300	      const { WorldGenClient } = await import('./workers/WorldGenClient');
301	      const client = new WorldGenClient();
302	      if (await client.probe()) {
303	        try {
304	          // ★与创建世界同款 VUI 加载页(原版风 UIWorldLoadState;用户报"读档 UI 还是旧的")
305	          const loadState = new UIWorldLoadState();
306	          VUI.setState(loadState);
307	          const { world, player, save } = await client.loadSave({ key }, {
308	            onProgress: (phase, p) => loadState.setProgress(loadProgressLabel(phase, p), p),
309	          });
310	          const g = makeGame();
311	          applyDifficultyFallback(world, save as { header?: { difficulty?: number } } | undefined);
312	          await g.loadWorld(world, (label, p) => loadState.setProgress(label, 0.8 + p * 0.2), { settled: true });
313	          applyPlayer(g, player as ReturnType<typeof loadSaveData>['player']);
314	          g.eventsApplySave((save as { events?: never } | undefined)?.events as never);
315	          return;
316	        } catch (e) {
317	          if (!(e instanceof (await import('./workers/WorldGenClient')).WorldGenUnavailable)) throw e;
318	          // worker 失败 → 主线程 fallback
319	        }
320	      }
321	      const text = await kvGet(key);
322	      if (!text) { ui.toast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.SaveLoadFailed', '存档数据缺失')); return; }
323	      await loadFromJson(text);
324	    } catch (e) {
325	      console.error(e);
326	      ui.hideProgress();
327	      VUI.setState(null);   // 加载页退场(失败回菜单)
328	      alert(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.SaveLoadFailed', (e as Error).message));
329	    }
330	  }
331	
332	  async function loadFromJson(text: string) {
333	    try {
334	      // worker 路径：JSON 解析 + RLE 解码 + load 模式沉降全在后台（json 源传入）
335	      const { WorldGenClient } = await import('./workers/WorldGenClient');
336	      const client = new WorldGenClient();
337	      if (await client.probe()) {
338	        try {
339	          const loadState = new UIWorldLoadState();
340	          VUI.setState(loadState);
341	          const { world, player, save } = await client.loadSave({ json: text }, {
342	            onProgress: (phase, p) => loadState.setProgress(loadProgressLabel(phase, p), p),
343	          });
344	          const g = makeGame();
345	          applyDifficultyFallback(world, save as { header?: { difficulty?: number } } | undefined);
346	          await g.loadWorld(world, (label, p) => loadState.setProgress(label, 0.8 + p * 0.2), { settled: true });
347	          applyPlayer(g, player as ReturnType<typeof loadSaveData>['player']);
348	          g.eventsApplySave((save as { events?: never } | undefined)?.events as never);
349	          return;
350	        } catch (e) {
351	          if (!(e instanceof (await import('./workers/WorldGenClient')).WorldGenUnavailable)) throw e;
352	          // worker 失败 → 主线程 fallback
353	        }
354	      }
355	      const { world, player, data } = loadSave(text);
356	      applyDifficultyFallback(world, data);
357	      const g = makeGame();
358	      const loadState = new UIWorldLoadState();
359	      VUI.setState(loadState);
360	      loadState.setProgress(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Progress.LoadingSave'), 0.3);
361	      await g.loadWorld(world, (label, p) => loadState.setProgress(label, 0.3 + p * 0.6));
362	      applyPlayer(g, player);
363	      g.eventsApplySave(data.events);
364	    } catch (e) {
365	      console.error(e);
366	      ui.hideProgress();
367	      VUI.setState(null);   // 加载页退场(失败回菜单)
368	      alert(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.SaveLoadFailed', (e as Error).message));
369	    }
370	  }
371	
372	  async function importWld(buf: Uint8Array) {
373	    ui.showProgress(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Progress.ParsingWld'), 0.1);
374	    try {
375	      const { save, report, seedText, gameMode } = parseWldToSave(buf);
376	      (window as unknown as { __lastCompatReport?: unknown }).__lastCompatReport = report;
377	      ui.showProgress(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Progress.ConvertingWld'), 0.7);
378	      const g = makeGame();
379	      // 直接消费内存 SaveData(2026-08 审计 #3:此前的 stringify→parse 双拷贝
380	      // 每次导入多出 2×20~50MB 峰值,且让 5 份全图副本并存更久)
381	      const { world } = loadSaveData(save);
382	      await g.loadWorld(world, (label, p) => ui.showProgress(label, 0.7 + p * 0.3));
383	      g.player.inv.add(ITEM_BY_KEY[VI_KEY.STARTER.PICK], 1);
384	      g.player.inv.add(ITEM_BY_KEY[VI_KEY.STARTER.AXE], 1);
385	      g.player.inv.add(ITEM_BY_KEY[VI_KEY.STARTER.SHORTSWORD], 1);
386	      g.player.inv.add(ITEM_BY_KEY[VI_KEY.TORCH], 20);
387	      ui.toast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.WldImported', save.header.name ?? '', save.header.wldVersion ?? 0));
388	      // 登记世界槽位并持久化：导入不再是一次性的，重进游戏可在世界列表中看到并继续游玩
389	      await worldStore.ensureLoaded();
390	      const names = new Set(worldStore.list().map((m) => m.name));
391	      let name = save.header.name;
392	      if (names.has(name)) {
393	        let i = 2;
394	        while (names.has(`${name} (${i})`)) i++;
395	        name = `${name} (${i})`;
396	      }
397	      currentWorld = await worldStore.register({
398	        name, seed: seedText || String(save.header.seed),
399	        w: save.header.width, h: save.header.height,
400	        difficulty: gameMode, evil: save.header.crimson ? 1 : 0,
401	      });
402	      doSave();
403	      // 兼容报告：有降级/跳过内容时弹窗
404	      const rpt = (window as unknown as { __lastCompatReport?: import('./ui/UI').CompatReport }).__lastCompatReport;
405	      if (rpt && (rpt.tilesDegraded.length || rpt.tilesCleared.length || rpt.itemsSkipped.length)) {
406	        ui.showCompatReport(rpt);
407	      }
408	    } catch (e) {
409	      console.error(e);
410	      ui.hideProgress();
411	      alert(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.WldImportFailed', (e as Error).message));
412	    }
413	  }

417	  /** 帧回调注入 VUI 自愈循环（VUI.startLoop 持有 rAF，HMR 杀不死） */
418	  VUI.frameHook = (dt) => {

433	  /** 加入房间（探针桥 + UI 共用语义）：url = ws://host:port/<房间码>[?token=] */
434	  async function joinRoom(url: string, hostToken = ''): Promise<void> {
435	    await makeGame().joinNetGame(url, () => {}, hostToken);
436	  }
437	
438	  /** 探针桥：当前世界序列化后向中央服务器建房（返回 {code, hostToken}） */
439	  async function createRoom(addr: string, opts: { name?: string; public?: boolean; protectTiles?: boolean; protectItems?: boolean; maxPlayers?: number } = {}): Promise<{ code: string; hostToken: string; error?: string }> {
440	    try {
441	      if (!game) return { error: 'no game', code: '', hostToken: '' };
442	      const { saveGame } = await import('./save/SaveFile');
443	      await worldStore.ensureLoaded();
444	      if (!currentWorld) {
445	        currentWorld = await worldStore.register({
446	          name: game.world.name, seed: String(game.world.seed), w: game.world.w, h: game.world.h,
447	          difficulty: 0, evil: game.world.crimson ? 1 : 0,
448	        });
449	      }
450	      const json = saveGame(game.world, game.player, Date.now() - playStart, game.townNpcsForSave(), game.eventsForSave());
451	      await worldStore.save(currentWorld, json, Date.now() - playStart);
452	      const lobbyPort = addr.includes(':') ? parseInt(addr.split(':')[1], 10) : 7778;
453	      const host = addr.includes(':') ? addr.split(':')[0] : addr;
454	      const r = await fetch(`http://${host}:${lobbyPort}/rooms`, {
455	        method: 'POST',
456	        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
457	        body: JSON.stringify({
458	          name: opts.name ?? game.world.name,
459	          public: opts.public ?? true,
460	          protectTiles: !!opts.protectTiles,
461	          protectItems: !!opts.protectItems,
462	          maxPlayers: opts.maxPlayers,
463	          save: json,
464	        }),
465	      });
466	      const data = await r.json() as { ok: boolean; code?: string; hostToken?: string; error?: string };
467	      if (!data.ok || !data.code || !data.hostToken) return { error: data.error ?? 'bad response', code: '', hostToken: '' };
468	      return { code: data.code, hostToken: data.hostToken };
469	    } catch (e) {
470	      return { error: String((e as Error).message ?? e), code: '', hostToken: '' };
471	    }
472	  }
473	
474	  function showMultiplayerSelect() {
475	    ui.closeAll();
476	    titleMenu?.destroy();
477	    titleMenu = null;
478	    let panelRoot: HTMLElement | null = null;
479	    const closePanel = () => { panelRoot?.remove(); panelRoot = null; };
480	    const panel = new MultiplayerSelect({
481	      // v3 房间制：onJoinRoom(url, hostToken)——url 含 /<房间码>[?token=]
482	      onJoinRoom: (url, hostToken) => {
483	        closePanel();
484	        ui.showProgress(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Progress.Connecting'), 0.1);
485	        const g = makeGame();
486	        // 连接前应用选中角色：Hello 的玩家名（加入公告"X已加入。"）与首包
487	        // SyncPlayer 外观都取自 player.appearance——等 applyAppearance 就太晚了
488	        if (selectedAppearance) g.player.appearance = selectedAppearance;
489	        g.joinNetGame(url, (label, p) => {
490	          ui.showProgress(label, 0.1 + p * 0.8);
491	        }, hostToken).then(() => {
492	          // loadWorld 内部已 onWorldReady（enterGame 由 makeGame 回调完成）
493	        }, (e) => {
494	          ui.hideProgress();
495	          ui.toast(`联机失败：${(e as Error).message}`);
496	          showTitle();
497	        });
498	      },
499	      onBack: () => { closePanel(); showTitle(); },
500	      listSaves: async () => {
501	        // 列出世界槽位并读出存档 JSON（IndexedDB → 上传给中央服务器建房）
502	        await worldStore.ensureLoaded();
503	        return Promise.all(worldStore.list().map(async (m) => ({
504	          id: m.id,
505	          name: m.name,
506	          json: await worldStore.load(m),
507	        })));
508	      },
509	      // 角色选择（需求："可以选择一个角色加入房间"）——选中后 applyAppearance 补发外观
510	      listCharacters: async () => {
511	        await charStore.ensureLoaded();
512	        return charStore.list().map((c) => ({ id: c.id, name: c.name }));
513	      },
514	      onPickCharacter: async (id: number) => {
515	        selectedAppearance = await charStore.get(id);
516	        selectedCharId = id;
517	      },
518	    });
519	    panelRoot = panel.root;
520	    root.appendChild(panelRoot);
521	  }
522

531	        selectedCharId = id ?? null;

541	        });
542	      },

547	  /** 世界列表（DOM） */
548	  function showWorldSelect() {
549	    VUI.setState(null);
550	    VUI.clear();
551	    new WorldSelectPanel(root, worldStore, {
552	      onPlay: (meta) => void loadWorldFlow(meta),
553	      onNew: () => {
554	        // 世界创建页（DOM）
555	        VUI.setState(null);
556	        VUI.clear();
557	        new WorldCreationPanel(root, {
558	          onCreate: (cfg) => void createWorldFlow(cfg),
559	          onCancel: () => showWorldSelect(),
560	        });
561	      },

565	
566	  /** 从世界槽位读取并进入游戏（worker 内直读 IDB：免大 JSON 字符串主线程读取
567	   *  + 结构化克隆双开销；fallback 时 worldStore.load 取回全文走 loadFromJson） */
568	  async function loadWorldFlow(meta: WorldMeta) {
569	    currentWorld = meta;
570	    // 旧档（无 header.difficulty）以槽位 meta 回填世界难度；finally 清防泄漏到
571	    // 后续 quickLoad/文件导入路径（那些场景 fallback=null 不生效）
572	    difficultyFallback = meta.difficulty;
573	    try {
574	      await loadFromKey(`sandboxworld.world.${meta.id}`);
575	    } finally {
576	      difficultyFallback = null;
577	    }
578	  }
579	
580	  /** 创建新世界：原版生成页（双进度条+实时地图预览）→ 注册槽位 → 进游戏 */
581	  async function createWorldFlow(cfg: { name: string; seed: string; w: number; h: number; difficulty: number; evil: -1 | 0 | 1 }) {
582	    const loadState = new UIWorldLoadState(cfg.evil);
583	    VUI.setState(loadState);
584	    const g = makeGame();
585	    await g.newWorld(cfg.seed || String(Date.now()), cfg.w, cfg.h,
586	      (label, p) => loadState.setProgress(label, p),
587	      {
588	        name: cfg.name,
589	        evil: cfg.evil,
590	        difficulty: cfg.difficulty,   // 世界难度 = Main.GameMode（Main.cs:2677；创建 UI 四档）
591	        onWorldPartial: (world) => loadState.attachWorld(world), // 主线程 fallback：列扫描预览
592	        onPreview: (f) => loadState.attachPreview(f),            // worker 路径：位图预览
593	      });
594	    // onWorldReady 已进游戏；登记世界槽位并保存初始数据
595	    const meta = await worldStore.register({
596	      name: cfg.name, seed: cfg.seed, w: cfg.w, h: cfg.h,
597	      difficulty: cfg.difficulty, evil: cfg.evil,
598	    });
599	    currentWorld = meta;
600	    doSave();
601	  }
602	
603	  /** 设置面板（主菜单含数据栏；游戏内不显示数据栏） */
604	  function openSettings(inGame: boolean) {
605	    new SettingsPanel(root, {
606	      ...(inGame ? {} : {
607	        onQuickLoad: () => void quickLoad(),
608	        onLoadFile: () => {
609	          fileInput.onchange = () => {
610	            const f = fileInput.files?.[0];
611	            if (f) void f.text().then(loadFromJson);
612	            fileInput.value = '';
613	          };
614	          fileInput.click();
615	        },
616	        onImportWld: () => {
617	          wldInput.onchange = () => {
618	            const f = wldInput.files?.[0];
619	            if (f) void f.arrayBuffer().then((ab) => importWld(new Uint8Array(ab)));
620	            wldInput.value = '';
621	          };
622	          wldInput.click();
623	        },
624	      }),
625	      onBack: () => { /* 面板自毁 */ },
626	    });
627	  }
628	
629	  /** 图鉴面板（UIBestiaryTest 1:1 结构：网格分页/筛选排序/条目卡/完成度条）。
630	   *  打开期间暂停世界并阻断输入；Esc/E 关闭后恢复先前的暂停/阻断态
631	   *  （从暂停菜单进入时其面板仍叠在下层，关闭即回暂停菜单）。 */
632	  function openBestiary() {
633	    const g = game;
634	    if (!g) return;
635	    const wasPaused = g.paused;
636	    const wasBlocking = g.input.uiBlocking;
637	    g.paused = true;
638	    g.input.uiBlocking = true;
639	    new BestiaryPanel(root, {
640	      getTracker: () => g.world.bestiary,
641	      atlas,
642	      isCrimson: () => g.world.crimson,
643	      onBack: () => {
644	        if (!wasPaused) g.paused = false;
645	        if (!wasBlocking) g.input.uiBlocking = false;
646	      },
647	    });
648	  }
649	
650	  function showTitle() {
651	    audio.play('title');
652	    ui.closeAll();
653	    if (!menuBg) {
654	      menuBg = new MenuBackground(root);
655	      (window as unknown as { __swMenuBg?: MenuBackground }).__swMenuBg = menuBg; // 调试句柄
656	    }
657	    titleMenu?.destroy();
658	    titleMenu = new TitleMenu(root, {
659	      onSinglePlayer: () => showCharacterSelect(),
660	      onSettings: () => openSettings(false),
661	      onQuit: () => ui.toast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.QuitUnsupported')),
662	      onCredits: () => ui.toast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.CreditsLine')),
663	      onMultiplayer: () => showMultiplayerSelect(),
664	    });
665	    installLegacyShim();
666	  }
667	
668	  /** 旧 puppeteer 脚本兼容垫片：select(尺寸)+button(创建) 隐藏 DOM（M7 移除）。
669	   *  挂 root 末尾（在天空画布之上）；标题页按钮在屏幕中央不与垫片（左上角）重叠，
670	   *  querySelector('button') 仍命中垫片——旧探针脚本零修改。 */
671	  function installLegacyShim() {
672	    if (legacyShim) return;
673	    const div = document.createElement('div');
674	    div.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;left:0;top:0;width:2px;height:2px;opacity:0.01;z-index:1;';
675	    const sel = document.createElement('select');
676	    sel.innerHTML = '<option value="4200x1200">小</option><option value="6400x1800" selected>中</option><option value="8400x2400">大</option>';
677	    const btn = document.createElement('button');
678	    btn.textContent = '创建新世界';
679	    btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
680	      const [w, h] = (sel.value || '6400x1800').split('x').map(Number);
681	      void newWorld('', w, h);
682	    });
683	    div.append(sel, btn);
684	    root.appendChild(div);
685	    legacyShim = div;
686	  }
687	
688	  function quitToMenu() {
689	    mobile?.destroy();
690	    mobile = null;
691	    game?.destroy();
692	    game = null;
693	    currentWorld = null;
694	    ui.closeAll();
695	    showTitle();
696	  }
697	
698	  function doSave() {
699	    if (!game) return;
700	    // 存档序列化走后台 worker(RLE+base64+JSON 全套离主线程;主线程只付一次
701	    // 全图数组结构化克隆的 memcpy),不可用时 SaveClient 内部回退同步 saveGame
702	    saveClient.ensure();
703	    void saveClient.save(game.world, game.player, Date.now() - playStart, game.townNpcsForSave(), game.eventsForSave()).then((json) => {
704	      const mb = json.length / 1024 / 1024;
705	      void kvSetCompat(json).then((where) => {
706	        if (where === 'fail') ui.toast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.SaveFailedStorage'));
707	        else {
708	          quickSaveExists = true; // 同会话存档后 quickLoad 立即可用（此前闭包标志只在建流时查一次）
709	          ui.toast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.Saved', where === 'idb' ? mb.toFixed(1) + ' MB → IndexedDB' : Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Save.Local')));
710	        }
711	      }).catch((e) => {
712	        console.error('kvSet failed', e);
713	        ui.toast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.SaveFailedStorage'));
714	      });
715	      // 多世界槽位（M3）：登记到 WorldStore
716	      if (currentWorld) {
717	        void worldStore.save(currentWorld, json, Date.now() - playStart).catch((e) => console.error('worldStore.save failed', e));
718	      }
719	    }).catch((e) => {
720	      // 回退同步 saveGame 也失败(异常世界状态)——如实报告,不留 unhandled rejection
721	      console.error('save failed', e);
722	      ui.toast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.SaveFailedStorage'));
723	    });
724	  }
725	
726	  /** 导出存档:同一序列化链(saveClient),产物落盘为 .sbw.json 下载——
727	   *  与 __swFlow.loadJson / 世界槽位导入互为闭环,便于备份与跨机迁移 */
728	  function doExportSave() {
729	    if (!game) return;
730	    saveClient.ensure();
731	    void saveClient.save(game.world, game.player, Date.now() - playStart, game.townNpcsForSave(), game.eventsForSave()).then((json) => {
732	      const name = (game!.world.name ?? 'world').replace(/\s+/g, '_');
733	      const d = new Date();
734	      const p = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
735	      const filename = `${name}-${d.getFullYear()}${p(d.getMonth() + 1)}${p(d.getDate())}-${p(d.getHours())}${p(d.getMinutes())}.sbw.json`;
736	      const blob = new Blob([json], { type: 'application/json' });
737	      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
738	      const a = document.createElement('a');
739	      a.href = url; a.download = filename;
740	      a.click();
741	      setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 10_000);
742	      ui.toast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.Exported', filename));
743	    }).catch((e) => {
744	      console.error('export save failed', e);
745	      ui.toast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.SaveFailedStorage'));
746	    });
747	  }
748	
749	  // KvStore 延迟引入避免顶层副作用顺序问题
750	  async function kvSetCompat(json: string): Promise<string> {
751	    const { kvSet } = await import('./save/KvStore');
752	    return kvSet(QUICK_SAVE_KEY, json);
753	  }
754	
755	  // 快速存档存在性预查
756	  void kvHas(QUICK_SAVE_KEY).then((v) => { quickSaveExists = v; });
757	
758	  // 调试桥（puppeteer 脚本用）
759	  (window as unknown as { __swFlow?: unknown }).__swFlow = {
760	    newWorld: (seed: string, w: number, h: number) => newWorld(seed, w, h),
761	    quickLoad,
762	    importWld,
763	    quitToMenu,
764	    doSave,
765	    doExportSave,
766	    backToTitle: showTitle,
767	    devMode: () => devMode,
768	    joinGame: (ip: string) => joinRoom(ip.includes(':') ? `ws://${ip}` : `ws://${ip}:7777`), // 旧桥（直连 URL）
769	    joinRoom: (url: string, token: string) => joinRoom(url, token), // 探针桥：房间制加入
770	    createRoom: (addr: string, opts: Record<string, unknown>) => createRoom(addr, opts as never), // 探针桥：建房
771	    loadJson: (text: string) => loadFromJson(text), // 读自有 json 存档（探针）
772	    itemByKey: (key: string) => ITEM_BY_KEY[key] ?? -1, // 探针桥：物品 id（生产构建也可用——页内 import('/src/...') 仅 dev 可达）
773	    spawnVanillaEnemy: async (id: number, x: number, y: number) => {
774	      const { Enemy } = await import('./entities/Enemy');
775	      if (!game) return null;
776	      const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(id, x, y);
777	      if (!e) return null;
778	      game.entities.add(e, 'enemies');
779	      return { id: e.id, x: e.x, y: e.y, hp: e.hp };
780	    },
781	    /** 探针桥：敌对弹幕（原版 Projectile.hostile）——v5 msg27 bit7 链路 + 访客 Damage_EVP 本地结算的 E2E 验证。
782	     *  grav 默认 0（直飞激光语义；Arrow 类默认 0.3 抛物线会让探针弹坠离目标） */
783	    spawnHostileProj: async (x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, dmg: number, projId = 9, grav = 0) => {
784	      const { Arrow } = await import('./entities/Arrow');
785	      if (!game) return null;
786	      const p = new Arrow(x, y, vx, vy, dmg, 0, projId, null, { hostile: true, grav });
787	      game.entities.add(p, 'projectiles');
788	      return { id: p.id };
789	    },
790	  };
791	
792	  return {
793	    showTitle,
794	    newWorld,
795	    quickLoad,
796	    importWld,
797	    quitToMenu,
798	    doSave,
799	    doExportSave,
800	    openSettings,
801	    openBestiary,
802	    get game() { return game; },
803	    playStart: 0,
804	    get playStartNow() { return Date.now() - playStart; },
805	  } as FlowHandle & { playStartNow: number };
806	}
807	

```

```
134	export class Sfx {
135	  private ctx: AudioContext | null = null;
136	  private buffers = new Map<string, AudioBuffer>();
137	  /** 在途解码中的文件（防重复 fetch） */
138	  private pending = new Set<string>();
139	  /** 循环轨在途解码（Promise 去重，与 pending 互斥使用） */
140	  private bufferPromises = new Map<string, Promise<AudioBuffer | null>>();
141	  /** 取回失败/不存在的文件（负缓存，防 404 每次重试） */
142	  private failed = new Set<string>();
143	  muted = false;
144	  /** 主音量 0..1（M6 设置页） */
145	  master = 1;
146	
147	  /** 按需取缓冲：未命中时后台 fetch+decode，同步返回已就位缓冲或 null */
148	  private ensureBuffer(file: string): AudioBuffer | null {
149	    const buf = this.buffers.get(file);
150	    if (buf) return buf;
151	    if (this.pending.has(file) || this.failed.has(file)) return null;
152	    const ctx = this.ensure(true);
153	    if (!ctx) return null;
154	    this.pending.add(file);
155	    fetch(`sounds/${file}.wav`)
156	      .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.arrayBuffer() : null))
157	      .then((ab) => (ab ? ctx.decodeAudioData(ab) : null))
158	      .then((decoded) => {
159	        if (decoded) this.buffers.set(file, decoded);
160	        else this.failed.add(file);
161	      })
162	      .catch(() => { this.failed.add(file); })
163	      .finally(() => { this.pending.delete(file); });
164	    return null;
165	  }
166	
167	  /** 定向预热（不阻塞）：把指定 wav 后台取齐，播放时即命中 */
168	  preloadFiles(files: Iterable<string>) {
169	    for (const f of files) this.ensureBuffer(f);
170	  }
171	
172	  /** 按逻辑名预热（WAV_MAP 展开到具体文件） */
173	  preloadNames(names: SfxName[]) {
174	    for (const n of names) for (const f of WAV_MAP[n] ?? []) this.ensureBuffer(f);
175	  }
176	
177	  // ---- 雨声层：原版 RainAmbience（Music_28.mp3，XACT Wave Bank 同源提取）循环 ----
178	  private rainGain: GainNode | null = null;
179	  private rainSrc: AudioBufferSourceNode | null = null;
180	  private rainLoaded = false;
181	  /** 设置雨声强度 0-1（每帧调用；0 时静音不拆节点——重启有冷启动开销） */
182	  setRain(vol: number) {
183	    const ctx = this.ensure(true);
184	    if (!ctx) return;
185	    if (!this.rainGain) {
186	      const g = ctx.createGain();
187	      g.gain.value = 0;
188	      g.connect(ctx.destination);
189	      this.rainGain = g;
190	      // 原版雨环境音轨（RainAmbience）懒加载循环；加载失败静默（雨声可选）
191	      fetch('audios/music/Music_28.mp3')
192	        .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.arrayBuffer() : null))
193	        .then((ab) => (ab ? ctx.decodeAudioData(ab) : null))
194	        .then((buf) => {
195	          if (!buf) return;
196	          const src = ctx.createBufferSource();
197	          src.buffer = buf;
198	          src.loop = true;
199	          src.connect(this.rainGain!);
200	          src.start();
201	          this.rainSrc = src;
202	          this.rainLoaded = true;
203	        })
204	        .catch(() => {});
205	    }
206	    const v = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, vol)) * this.master * 0.6;
207	    this.rainGain.gain.setTargetAtTime(v, ctx.currentTime, 0.4);
208	  }
209	
210	  /** 世界声距离衰减（LegacySoundPlayer.cs:160 SoundAttenuationDistance = 2500px） */
211	  static readonly ATTEN = 2500;
212	  /** 监听器（原版 Main.Camera.Center，相机中心非玩家）；Game 每帧更新。未进世界=(0,0) */
213	  private lx = 0; private ly = 0;
214	  setListener(x: number, y: number) { this.lx = x; this.ly = y; }
215	  /** 原版距离衰减（LegacySoundPlayer.cs:381-401）：dist ≥2500 → 不播；
216	   *  vol × (1 - dist/2500)。不传坐标（x=-1 语义）→ 全量（UI/玩家自身动作声） */
217	  private atten(vol: number, x?: number, y?: number): number {
218	    if (x == null || y == null) return vol;
219	    const d = Math.hypot(x - this.lx, y - this.ly);
220	    if (d >= Sfx.ATTEN) return 0;
221	    return vol * (1 - d / Sfx.ATTEN);
222	  }
223	
224	  private ensure(silent = false): AudioContext | null {
225	    if (this.muted && !silent) return null;
226	    if (!this.ctx) {
227	      const AC = window.AudioContext ?? (window as unknown as { webkitAudioContext: typeof AudioContext }).webkitAudioContext;
228	      if (!AC) return null;
229	      this.ctx = new AC();
230	    }
231	    if (this.ctx.state === 'suspended') this.ctx.resume().catch(() => {});
232	    return this.ctx;
233	  }
234	
235	  /** 播放原版 wav（随机变体 + 轻微音高扰动）；vol 0-1 */
236	  playWav(name: SfxName, vol = 1, x?: number, y?: number): boolean {
237	    const list = WAV_MAP[name];
238	    if (!list || !list.length) return false;
239	    return this.playWavFile(list[Math.floor(Math.random() * list.length)], vol, x, y);
240	  }
241	
242	  /** 播放任意 wav 文件列表（随机选 + 音高扰动）；供怪物专属声效用 */
243	  playFiles(files: string[], vol = 1, x?: number, y?: number): boolean {
244	    if (!files.length) return false;
245	    return this.playWavFile(files[Math.floor(Math.random() * files.length)], vol, x, y);
246	  }
247	
248	  /** 跟踪式循环音（SoundEngine.PlayTrackedLoopedSound 语义，AI_016 :44276 引信嘶声）:
249	   *  返回句柄 {stop, setPos}——setPos 按距离衰减刷新音量（ProjectileAudioTracker
250	   *  跟随弹体）。缓冲未就绪返回 null（调用方下 tick 重试即可）。 */
251	  startTrackedLoop(name: SfxName, vol: number, x: number, y: number): { stop(): void; setPos(px: number, py: number): void } | null {
252	    const list = WAV_MAP[name];
253	    if (!list || !list.length) return null;
254	    const ctx = this.ensure();
255	    if (!ctx) return null;
256	    const buf = this.ensureBuffer(list[0]);
257	    if (!buf) return null;
258	    const src = ctx.createBufferSource();
259	    src.buffer = buf;
260	    src.loop = true;
261	    src.playbackRate.value = 0.92 + Math.random() * 0.16;
262	    const g = ctx.createGain();
263	    g.gain.value = this.atten(vol, x, y) * this.master;
264	    src.connect(g).connect(ctx.destination);
265	    src.start();
266	    return {
267	      stop: () => { try { src.stop(); } catch { /* 已停 */ } g.disconnect(); },
268	      setPos: (px: number, py: number) => {
269	        g.gain.value = this.atten(vol, px, py) * this.master;
270	      },
271	    };
272	  }
273	
274	  // ---- 环境循环轨（SoundEngine.PlayTrackedSound 语义：常驻节点 + 音量平滑跟随）----
275	
276	  /** 异步取缓冲（ensureBuffer 的 Promise 版，供循环轨"缓冲就绪后再起振"） */
277	  private ensureBufferAsync(file: string, ctx: AudioContext): Promise<AudioBuffer | null> {
278	    const hit = this.buffers.get(file);
279	    if (hit) return Promise.resolve(hit);
280	    if (this.failed.has(file)) return Promise.resolve(null);
281	    const pending = this.bufferPromises.get(file);
282	    if (pending) return pending;
283	    const p = fetch(`sounds/${file}.wav`)
284	      .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.arrayBuffer() : null))
285	      .then((ab) => (ab ? ctx.decodeAudioData(ab) : null))
286	      .then((decoded) => {
287	        if (decoded) { this.buffers.set(file, decoded); return decoded; }
288	        this.failed.add(file);
289	        return null;
290	      })
291	      .catch(() => { this.failed.add(file); return null; })
292	      .finally(() => { this.bufferPromises.delete(file); });
293	    this.bufferPromises.set(file, p);
294	    return p;
295	  }
296	
297	  /** 循环轨底层（任意 wav 文件 + 外部基础音量）；缓冲未就位时句柄先行返回，
298	   *  解码完成后自动起振——调用方无需重试 */
299	  private startLoopFile(file: string, vol: number, base: number): SfxLoop | null {
300	    const ctx = this.ensure();
301	    if (!ctx) return null;
302	    const gain = ctx.createGain();
303	    gain.gain.value = 0;
304	    gain.connect(ctx.destination);
305	    const st = {
306	      src: null as AudioBufferSourceNode | null,
307	      vol: Math.max(0, Math.min(1, vol)),
308	      base,
309	      started: false,
310	      stopped: false,
311	    };
312	    const apply = () => {
313	      const target = Math.min(1, st.vol * st.base * this.master);
314	      gain.gain.setTargetAtTime(target, ctx.currentTime, 0.2);
315	    };
316	    const handle: SfxLoop = {
317	      stop: () => {
318	        if (st.stopped) return;
319	        st.stopped = true;
320	        try { st.src?.stop(); } catch { /* 已停 */ }
321	        st.src?.disconnect();
322	        gain.disconnect();
323	      },
324	      setVolume: (v: number) => {
325	        st.vol = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, v));
326	        if (st.started && !st.stopped) apply();
327	      },
328	      get playing() { return st.started && !st.stopped; },
329	    };
330	    void this.ensureBufferAsync(file, ctx).then((buf) => {
331	      if (st.stopped || !buf) return;
332	      const src = ctx.createBufferSource();
333	      src.buffer = buf;
334	      src.loop = true;
335	      src.connect(gain);
336	      src.start();
337	      st.src = src;
338	      st.started = true;
339	      apply();
340	    });
341	    return handle;
342	  }
343	
344	  /**
345	   * 环境循环轨播放（SoundEngine.PlayTrackedSound 语义）：返回常驻句柄，
346	   * 音量经 setVolume 每帧跟随（天气状态机驱动）。默认音量 0.3（不吵）。
347	   * 无素材轨（如 sandstormWind）返回 null——调用方跳过即可，无需重试。
348	   */
349	  playLoop(name: WeatherLoopName, vol = 0.3): SfxLoop | null {
350	    const list = WAV_MAP[name];
351	    if (!list || !list.length) return null;
352	    return this.startLoopFile(list[0], vol, LOOP_BASE_VOL[name]);
353	  }
354	
355	  /** 按文件名播放循环（沙暴等未来素材接入的备用口） */
356	  playLoopFile(file: string, vol = 0.3, base = 1): SfxLoop | null {
357	    return this.startLoopFile(file, vol, base);
358	  }
359	
360	  /** 按文件名直播（缓冲未就位时触发后台加载，本播返回 false 走合成兜底） */
361	  playWavFile(file: string, vol = 1, x?: number, y?: number): boolean {
362	    const ctx = this.ensure();
363	    if (!ctx) return false;
364	    const v = this.atten(vol, x, y);
365	    if (v <= 0) return false;
366	    vol = v;
367	    const buf = this.ensureBuffer(file);
368	    if (!buf) return false;
369	    const src = ctx.createBufferSource();
370	    src.buffer = buf;
371	    src.playbackRate.value = 0.92 + Math.random() * 0.16;
372	    const g = ctx.createGain();
373	    g.gain.value = Math.min(1, vol * this.master);
374	    src.connect(g).connect(ctx.destination);
375	    src.start();
376	    return true;
377	  }
378	
379	  play(name: SfxName, vol = 1, x?: number, y?: number) {
380	    // 优先原版素材，失败回退合成（master 在 playWavFile/osc 各自生效一次）。
381	    // 带坐标时衰减（atten），≤0 直接不播——合成回退也不触发
382	    if (x != null && y != null && this.atten(vol, x, y) <= 0) return;
383	    if (this.playWav(name, vol, x, y)) return;
384	    const ctx = this.ensure();
385	    if (!ctx) return;
386	    const t = ctx.currentTime;
387	    const osc = (type: OscillatorType, f0: number, f1: number, dur: number, vol: number, delay = 0) => {
388	      const o = ctx.createOscillator();
389	      const g = ctx.createGain();
390	      o.type = type;
391	      o.frequency.setValueAtTime(f0, t + delay);
392	      o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(1, f1), t + delay + dur);
393	      g.gain.setValueAtTime(vol * this.master, t + delay);
394	      g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t + delay + dur);
395	      o.connect(g).connect(ctx.destination);
396	      o.start(t + delay);
397	      o.stop(t + delay + dur);
398	    };
399	    switch (name) {
400	      case 'hit': osc('square', 320, 110, 0.09, 0.12); break;
401	      case 'chop': osc('triangle', 220, 80, 0.12, 0.16); break;
402	      case 'hurt': osc('sawtooth', 180, 60, 0.22, 0.14); break;
403	      case 'killed': osc('square', 260, 60, 0.25, 0.14); break;
404	      case 'pkilled': osc('sawtooth', 160, 40, 0.6, 0.18); break;
405	      case 'zombie': osc('sawtooth', 90, 55, 0.5, 0.1); break;
406	      case 'roar': osc('sawtooth', 70, 40, 0.9, 0.2); break;
407	      case 'pickup': osc('sine', 520, 990, 0.09, 0.1); osc('sine', 780, 1320, 0.08, 0.06, 0.05); break;
408	      case 'dig': osc('square', 140, 90, 0.05, 0.06); break;
409	      case 'tink': osc('sine', 900, 500, 0.06, 0.08); break;
410	      case 'place': osc('triangle', 260, 120, 0.08, 0.12); break;
411	      case 'drink': osc('sine', 300, 620, 0.06, 0.08); osc('sine', 420, 780, 0.06, 0.07, 0.07); osc('sine', 560, 940, 0.06, 0.06, 0.14); break;
412	      case 'splash': osc('sine', 700, 200, 0.18, 0.1); break;
413	      case 'summon': {
414	        // 召唤杖 UseSound(Item_44)近似:短促"噗"——高频下扫正弦 + 一撮噪声气声
415	        osc('sine', 900, 260, 0.16, 0.14);
416	        osc('triangle', 1400, 500, 0.08, 0.06);
417	        break;
418	      }
419	      case 'whipCrack': {
420	        // 鞭响(Item_152)近似:高频噪声急促炸开 + 快速衰减
421	        const wDur = 0.12, wn = Math.floor(ctx.sampleRate * wDur);
422	        const wBuf = ctx.createBuffer(1, wn, ctx.sampleRate);
423	        const wd = wBuf.getChannelData(0);
424	        for (let i = 0; i < wn; i++) wd[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * Math.pow(1 - i / wn, 3);
425	        const wSrc = ctx.createBufferSource();
426	        wSrc.buffer = wBuf;
427	        const hp = ctx.createBiquadFilter();
428	        hp.type = 'highpass';
429	        hp.frequency.value = 1200;
430	        const wg = ctx.createGain();
431	        wg.gain.value = 0.18 * this.master;
432	        wSrc.connect(hp).connect(wg).connect(ctx.destination);
433	        wSrc.start(t);
434	        break;
435	      }
436	      case 'explosion': {
437	        // 噪声爆发近似(原版 Item_14):一次性白噪 buffer + 低频 thump。
438	        // ★曾无此分支:按需加载首播(wav 还在 fetch)完全静音 = "炸弹没有音效"主因
439	        const dur = 0.5, n = Math.floor(ctx.sampleRate * dur);
440	        const buf = ctx.createBuffer(1, n, ctx.sampleRate);
441	        const d = buf.getChannelData(0);
442	        for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) d[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * Math.pow(1 - i / n, 2.2);
443	        const src = ctx.createBufferSource();
444	        src.buffer = buf;
445	        const lp = ctx.createBiquadFilter();
446	        lp.type = 'lowpass';
447	        lp.frequency.setValueAtTime(900, t);
448	        lp.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(120, t + dur);
449	        const g = ctx.createGain();
450	        g.gain.value = 0.5 * this.master;
451	        src.connect(lp).connect(g).connect(ctx.destination);
452	        src.start(t);
453	        osc('sine', 90, 35, 0.35, 0.35); // 低频冲击
454	        break;
455	      }
456	      case 'coin': osc('sine', 990, 1400, 0.07, 0.08); osc('sine', 1320, 1760, 0.09, 0.06, 0.05); break;
457	      case 'door_open': osc('triangle', 180, 320, 0.12, 0.1); break;
458	      case 'door_close': osc('triangle', 320, 160, 0.1, 0.1); break;
459	      case 'shatter': osc('square', 1400, 300, 0.15, 0.1); break;
460	      case 'drown': osc('sine', 400, 90, 0.4, 0.1); break;
461	      case 'mirror': osc('sine', 700, 1900, 0.3, 0.06); osc('sine', 1400, 2800, 0.25, 0.04, 0.1); break;   // 魔镜闪烁上行
462	      case 'conch': osc('sine', 220, 330, 0.25, 0.08); osc('sine', 330, 440, 0.3, 0.06, 0.2); break;        // 海螺低鸣
463	    }
464	  }
465	}
```
